- **.overview**: 本目录是华为云 FCAPS 诊断技能的命令契约库，按故障、配置、计量计费、性能、安全和共享查询组织，再按 hcloud、Linux、Windows 与可选 Kubernetes 区分事实所在环境。它用于从已知问题或完整命令 ID 定位操作、直接观测、输入依赖与执行约束，覆盖云资源、主机及集群；场景步骤、工具条件和参数映射分别由相邻资料提供。目录中也包含变更、扫描和主动采集，选读时须核对实际作用与授权。 ([concept-git/cloud-concept-skills](https://github.com/concept-git/cloud-concept-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/drafts/huawei-cloud-fcaps-diagnostics/references/commands/.overview.md) · ★5)

- **/a11y-audit**: Auditoría de accesibilidad WCAG 2.2 completa con escaneo de HTML/componentes. - **10x-loop**: Run one plan-driven work turn: take the first todo phase from the plan file, implement it, prove it with a mutation-checked gate, commit atomically, stop. Never pushes. State lives in the plan file, so the loop resumes after a context clear. ([arhuman/claude-plugins](https://github.com/arhuman/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/10x-loop.md))
- **10x-make-conform**: Bring a repo up to the 10x engineering standard: diagnose drift, then dispatch the manifest remediations (Makefile, CI, Docker, versioning, governance, security headers, supply chain) and commit the fixes. - **10x-manual**: What 10x offers and where this project stands: the commands, the concepts, the files the system manages, plus the current plan, its phases, and what the verifiers say. - **10x-plan**: Steering documentation for agentic work: bootstrap the project scaffold and plan, add or promote a phase, report status, file a cross-cutting rule in the right document, or audit the whole set for inconsistency. - **10x-plan**: 10x-value planning: interview → audit → multi-dimensional plan → adversarial review → roadmap ([fivesmallq/claude-10x-workflow](https://github.com/fivesmallq/claude-10x-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/10x-en/commands/10x-plan.md))
- **10x-propose-probe**: Capture a confirmed conformance false negative (a check passed, or none existed, while a real deployed incident happened anyway) as a draft addition to the 10x engineering standard.
